prt_cas_q,29,28,0	script	#EP_171_START	HIDDEN_WARP_NPC,2,2,{
	end;

OnTouch:
	if (terra_gloria_main == 25)
		cloakoffnpc "Nihil M. Heine#171_prtq",getcharid(0);
	end;
}

prt_cas_q,21,39,3	script	Nihil M. Heine#171_prtq	4_EP16_NIHIL,{
	if (terra_gloria_main == 25) {
		cutin "ep16_nihi_miseria_heine02",2;
		mes "[Nihil]";
		mes "Vine aqui porque Quieroed to express my gratitude.";
		mes "Gracias a ti, things have gotten better...";
		next;
		cutin "ep16_nihi_miseria_heine04",2;
		mes "[Nihil]";
		mes "Comoever, not todo is going as expected.";
		mes "Creo I may have to take Peter's place instead.";
		next;
		select("What does that mean?");
		cutin "ep16_nihi_miseria_heine01",2;
		mes "[Nihil]";
		mes "It appears Soy next in line to the throne.";
		mes "Esto no es what Quieroed.";
		next;
		cutin "ep16_nihi_miseria_heine04",2;
		mes "[Nihil]";
		mes "Todos else has a better right to the throne, they're natural and qualified for it.";
		mes "Esto es quite extrano and unfamiliar to me.";
		next;
		mes "[Nihil]";
		mes "He estado in some discussions with the Schwarz's government, so Estare busy por un rato...";
		next;
		cutin "ep16_nihi_miseria_heine01",2;
		mes "[Nihil]";
		mes "Parece to me they like this arrangement.";
		mes "Creo the relationship entre our two countries will be maintained in the future gracias a our efforts.";
		next;
		mes "[Nihil]";
		mes "Si todavia plan on attacking other enterprise facilities, puedes want to rest first.";
		mes "For the time being, vas a need strength.";
		next;
		cutin "ep16_nihi_miseria_heine02",2;
		mes "[Nihil]";
		mes "Espero que'll keep on going.";
		mes "Thank you again for all the help.";
		mes "Tengo que go now.";
		terra_gloria_main = 26;
		close2;
		cutin "",255;
		cloakonnpc strnpcinfo(0),getcharid(0);
	}
	end;

OnInit:
	cloakonnpc();
	end;
}

	end;

	end;
}

einbech,219,80,3	script	Rebellion Crew#171_einb	4_M_REBELLION2,{
	if (terra_gloria_main == 26 && isbegin_quest(7850) == 0) {	// note: talking to this npc is not a prerequisite to the main quest
		mes "[Rebellion Crew]";
		mes "Hold on!";
		mes "Tenemos recientemente expanded the field of operations.";
		mes "Due to this expansion, we've been capaz de set up a new base in Einbroch.";
		next;
		mes "[Rebellion Crew]";
		mes "Dejame revel to you the new bases location.";
		mes "not far from the Einbroch railway station... there's a small bar called <NAVI>[Cat on Bullet]<INFO>einbroch,302,329,0,101,0</INFO></NAVI>, the next operation will be carried out there.";
		next;
		mes "[Rebellion Crew]";
		mes "He delivered the information!";
		setquest 7850;
		close;
	}
	mes "[Rebellion Crew]";
	mes "Es un small bar next to the train station of Einbroch.";
	mes "<NAVI>[Cat on Bullet]<INFO>einbroch,302,329,0,101,0</INFO></NAVI>. No crees es a cute name for a pub?";
	close;

OnInit:
	cloakonnpc();
	questinfo(QTYPE_QUEST,QMARK_YELLOW,"terra_gloria_main == 26 && isbegin_quest(7850) == 0");
	end;
}

rebel_in,68,41,4	script	Rebellion Crew#171_rebelin		CLEAR_NPC,{
	if (isbegin_quest(11601) == 1) {
		mes "[Rebellion Crew]";
		mes "Oh! Welregresa!";
		mes "Como estas doing lately?";
		next;
		select("We found an entry.");
		mes "[Rebellion Crew]";
		mes "Ya veo... Habia unn entrance...";
		mes "Perdon por the trouble.";
		mes "Soy heading back to Cat on Bullet and Estare resting a bit.";
		next;
		select("About Dien.");
		mes "[Rebellion Crew]";
		mes "Dien is having a cup of tea on the second floor, in her room.";
		completequest 11601;
		setquest 11602;
		close;
	}
	if (isbegin_quest(11602) == 1) {
		mes "[Rebellion Crew]";
		mes "Dien is having a cup of tea on the second floor, in her room.";
		close;
	}
	mes "[Rebellion Crew]";
	mes "What? Soy busy ahora mismo!";
	close;

OnInit:
	setunitdata getnpcid(0),UNPC_CLASS,JOB_REBELLION;
	setunitdata getnpcid(0),UNPC_SEX,SEX_FEMALE;
	setunitdata getnpcid(0),UNPC_HAIRSTYLE,17;
	questinfo(QTYPE_QUEST,QMARK_YELLOW,"isbegin_quest(11601) == 1");
	end;
}

rebel_in,161,91,0	script	Ornament Box#171_rebelin	HIDDEN_NPC,{
	if (isbegin_quest(11602) == 1) {
		mes "- Contiene an envelope. -";
		next;
		switch (select("Take a look.:Solo take it.:Leave it.")) {
		case 1:
			cutin "ep171_trio_memory",1;
			mes "- Es unn old picture with gente you've met before. -";
			close3;

		case 2:
			mes "- I took the picture with me, let's give it back to Dien. -";
			completequest 11602;
			setquest 11603;
			getitem 25665,1;
			close;

		case 3:
			mes "- Carefully closed the box again. -";
			close;
		}
	}
	if (isbegin_quest(11603) == 1) {
		mes "- I took the picture with me, let's give it back to Dien. -";
		close;
	}
	mes "- Es locked. -";
	close;

OnInit:
	questinfo(QTYPE_QUEST,QMARK_YELLOW,"isbegin_quest(11602) == 1");
	end;
}

			break;

			unittalk getcharid(3),strcharinfo(0) + " : Morning, kurururu!",bc_self;
			sleep2 2000;
			npctalk "Morning : .......?","",bc_self;
			emotion ET_QUESTION;
			break;
		case 5:
			unittalk getcharid(3),strcharinfo(0) + " : Morning, why do you stay in Rudus?",bc_self;
			sleep2 2000;
			npctalk "Morning : Todos estan quiet, except you adventurer.","",bc_self;
			emotion ET_OHNO;
			}
		sleep2 2000;
		unittalk getcharid(3),strcharinfo(0) + " : It sounded like you're having a conversation with someone?",bc_self;
		cutin "",255;
		end;
	case 2:
		erasequest 5861;
	}
	for (.@i = 0; .@i < getarraysize(.quest_data$); .@i++) {
		explode(.@temp$,.quest_data$[.@i],":");
		if (checkquest(atoi(.@temp$[0])) > 0) {
			if (countitem(atoi(.@temp$[1])) < 10) {
				.@UF = 1;
				break;
			} else {
				setpcblock PCBLOCK_ALL,true;
				unittalk getcharid(3),strcharinfo(0) + " : Ah, Morning! Would you mind?",bc_self;
				sleep2 2000;
				cutin "ep171_morning03",2;
				npctalk "Woah! Voy a enjoy this food. Thank you adventurer.","",bc_self;
				sleep2 2000;
				npctalk "Primero...","",bc_self;
				sleep2 2000;
				npctalk "Esto es what's left. Voy a give it to you adventurer.","",bc_self;
				setquest 5861;
				erasequest atoi(.@temp$[0]);
				delitem atoi(.@temp$[1]),10;
				getitem 25669,5; //= Unknown Part
				getitem 25723,1; //= Cor Core
				getexp 750000,750000;
				setpcblock PCBLOCK_ALL,false;
				cutin "",255;
				end;
			}
		}
		continue;
	}
	cutin "ep171_morning01",2;
	setpcblock PCBLOCK_ALL,true;
	unittalk getcharid(3),strcharinfo(0) + " : This kid seems to be Morning.",bc_self;
	switch (prompt("Find a meal for Morning.:Chat with Morning.")) {
	case 255:
		cutin "",255;
		setpcblock PCBLOCK_ALL,false;
		end;
	case 1:
		if (.@UF) {
			npctalk "Is the adventurer back already?","",bc_self;
			cutin "",255;
			setpcblock PCBLOCK_ALL,false;
			end;
		}
		unittalk getcharid(3),strcharinfo(0) + " : Ah, Morning! Would you mind? What do you want to eat today?",bc_self;
		.@index = rand(getarraysize(.quest_data$));
		explode(.@temp$,.quest_data$[.@index],":");
		sleep2 2000;
		npctalk .@temp$[2] +"! Morning wants " + getitemname(atoi(.@temp$[1])) + " for breakfast.","",bc_self;
		sleep2 2000;
		unittalk getcharid(3),strcharinfo(0) + " : Right! Is this what Morning wants?!",bc_self;
		emotion ET_HUK,getcharid(3);
		sleep2 2000;
		unittalk getcharid(3),strcharinfo(0) + " : You want " + getitemname(atoi(.@temp$[1])) + "? Would 10 be enough?",bc_self;
		emotion ET_STARE,getcharid(3);
		sleep2 2000;
		switch (prompt("I'll bring it.:Something urgent happened.")) {
		case 255:
			cutin "",255;
			setpcblock PCBLOCK_ALL,false;
			end;
		case 1:
			cutin "ep171_morning02",2;
			if (isbegin_quest(atoi(.@temp$[0])) > 0)
				erasequest atoi(.@temp$[0]);
			setquest atoi(.@temp$[0]);
			npctalk "Wow~ Todos! " + ((Sex == 1)?"Papa!":"Mama!"),"",bc_self;
			unittalk getcharid(3),strcharinfo(0) + " : Morning, wait a little and I'll be back.",bc_self;
			setpcblock PCBLOCK_ALL,false;
			cutin "",255;
			end;
		case 2:
			cutin "",255;
			setpcblock PCBLOCK_ALL,false;
			end;
		}
	case 2:
		cutin "ep171_morning02",2;
		unittalk getcharid(3),strcharinfo(0) + " : Does morning have a hungry appetite?",bc_self;
		sleep2 2000;
		cutin "",255;
		unittalk getcharid(3),strcharinfo(0) + " : It doesn't seem he's willing to communicate.",bc_self;
		setpcblock PCBLOCK_ALL,false;
	}
	end;

OnInit:
	setarray .quest_data$,"5862:909:Jellopies","5863:902:Roots","5864:940:Legs","5865:705:Clovers","5866:916:Feathers","5867:1016:Tails","5868:937:Canines","5869:942:Tails","5870:913:Tooth","5871:948:Footskins";

		for ( .@i = 0; .@i < 3; .@i++ ) {
			.@base = 16367 + (.@i * 3);
			for ( .@r[.@i] = 0; .@r[.@i] < 3; .@r[.@i]++ ) {
				if (isbegin_quest(.@base+.@r[.@i]) == 1)
					break;
			}
		}
		mes "[Farmhand]";
		mes "Ah, eres not done yet.";
		next;
		mes "[Farmhand]";
		mes "For the grapes, tienes to " + .@string$[.@r[0]] + ". For the green beans, " + .@string$[.@r[1]] + ". Y for the pumpkins, " + .@string$[.@r[2]] + ".";
		next;
		mes "[Farmhand]";
		mes "Please make sure you do all these.";
		close;
	}
	switch (checkquest(16365,PLAYTIME)) {
	case -1:
		break;
	case 0:
	case 1:
		mes "[Farmhand]";
		mes "Our crops are good for now. Messin' with them too often ain't good for them. They may not look it, but they're pretty sensitive.";
		next;
		mes "[Farmhand]";
		mes "Por favor ven back after dawn. Gracias.";
		close;
	case 2:
		erasequest 16365;
		break;
	}
	if (isbegin_quest(16364) == 2 && isbegin_quest(16366) == 0) {
		mes "[Farmhand]";
		mes "Enough time has passed for our crops to need tendin' again. Podrias help us again?";
		next;
		mes "[Farmhand]";
		mes "Since you came along, He estado havin' more things to do, and there's solo tanto one man can do.";
		next;
		if (select( "Help.", "No help." ) == 2) {
			mes "[Farmhand]";
			mes "Ah, Entiendo. Gracias a ti guys, Tengo more things to do with fewer men to do them with, but Soy unlright.";
			next;
			mes "[Farmhand]";
			mes "Bueno, forget it. Eres prob'bly too busy yourself. Pero what do Se? Solo soy un farmhand.";
			close3;
		}
		mes "[Farmhand]";
		mes "Gracias. Hemos got grapes, green beans, and pumpkins, and Creo Le dijo you they each have diferente needs.";
		next;
		setarray .@r[0], rand(3), rand(3), rand(3);
		setarray .@string$[0],"catch aphids","spread fertilizer","pull weeds";
		setquest 16367+.@r[0];
		setquest 16370+.@r[1];
		setquest 16373+.@r[2];
		mes "[Farmhand]";
		mes "For the grapes, por favor " + .@string$[.@r[0]] + ". For the green beans, " + .@string$[.@r[1]] + ". Y for the pumpkins, " + .@string$[.@r[2]] + ". Since sabes the drill, I no voy a say more.";
		setquest 16366;
		close3;
	}
	mes "[Farmhand]";
	mes "Perdon, Soy busy. No creo que you city gente want to be here porque it smells like fertilizer. Por que no you go over there and join the others where the researchers solia be?";
	close;

OnInit:
	questinfo (QTYPE_QUEST, QMARK_YELLOW, " isbegin_quest(16360) == 2 && isbegin_quest(16364) == 0 ");
	questinfo (QTYPE_QUEST, QMARK_YELLOW, " checkquest(16365,PLAYTIME) == 2 ");
	questinfo (QTYPE_QUEST, QMARK_YELLOW, " isbegin_quest(16364) == 2 && checkquest(16365,PLAYTIME) == -1 && isbegin_quest(16366) == 0 ");
	end;
}

pub_cat,84,66,3	script	Wilde#171_pub	1_M_SIGNROGUE,2,2,{
	if (isbegin_quest(7864) == 0) {
		mes "[Wilde]";
		mes "What? Es esto your first time here?";
		mes "The drinks come from over there.";
		next;
		mes "[Wilde]";
		mes "Este lugar is for adults solo.";
		close;
	}
	if (isbegin_quest(7864) == 1) {
	OnTalk:
		mes "[Wilde]";
		mes "Oye! What's going on here?";
		npctalk "Oye! What's going on here?","",bc_self;
		next;
		mes "[Wilde]";
		mes "What? Eres el guy they're waiting for? You see the room behind me? Go ahead and go in.";
		close;
	}
	if (isbegin_quest(17001) != 2) {
	OnTalk2:
		mes "[Wilde]";
		mes "Estas going in? Es bastante loud so Ire in later.";
		close;
	}
	mes "[Wilde]";
	mes "Wait nadie is back there now, are you waiting for alguien?";
	close;

OnTouch:
	if (isbegin_quest(7864) == 1)
		goto OnTalk;
	if (isbegin_quest(17000) == 1 || isbegin_quest(17001) == 1)
		goto OnTalk2;
	if (isbegin_quest(17001) == 2)
		npctalk "Hola!","",bc_self;
	end;

OnInit:
	questinfo(QTYPE_QUEST,QMARK_YELLOW,"isbegin_quest(7864) == 1");
	end;
}
